The find palette appends a "Create page" row when no title matches, and Cmd-N is the same picker in create mode instead of a separate dialog. Tabs and the side pane use Zed's `ui::Tab`/`TabBar` with a small native pane model, because `workspace::Pane` requires a `Project` and `AppState`; Ctrl-Tab cycling replaces focused-tab arrow keys. The Pages and Daily browsers are virtualized lists instead of 60-row pages, and the Daily month field is folded into the text filter (an ISO month prefix such as `2026-10` filters the same rows). The Files tree sorts case-insensitively rather than with ICU `localeCompare`. The picker needs Zed's `AppDatabase`; `editor_host::init` installs a private in-memory one so no Zed state is written.

Tests: `navigation_tests.rs` (palette filtering by title, text, and date; Enter and Shift-Enter; create and refusals; stale-result rejection), `workspace_tests.rs` (history order and forward cut; tab cycle, close fallback, and side pane; favorites, tabs, and side pane surviving reopen with per-notebook isolation; deleted pages dropped on reopen; Files keyboard expand, collapse, open, and open beside; Pages namespace and text filters), and `daily_tests.rs` (configured layout reaching the index and the Daily browser with a duplicate date; Cmd-J opening the folder page and creating tomorrow's page from the layout; saving a layout rewriting settings and re-dating pages; invalid and read-only layouts refused). All app, core, and storage tests pass (171 app tests), with Clippy warnings denied for all three crates and `bun run test:reference` passing.

Batch C (worktree `agent-a4d17406e635c15f8`, rt `5ea5c22310ff`). Launching without a named page opens today's daily page (`daily_tests`). Search hits, tag, task, and reference rows, `{{tasks}}` rows, and followed block links open the page at their block through one reveal path (`editor_host/stored/reveal.rs`, `Notebook::show_page_at`); nested child rows open at their parent block because the index keeps no offsets for nested children. With Vim on, Insert-starting keys pressed outside a text field go to the last-used page editor (`insert_anywhere.rs`). The workspace file now also keeps, per notebook: each pane's reading position as the source line at the top of the view (`positions`, keyed `page:<id>` and `side:<id>`, following renames and dropped with deleted pages), the Pages filter and namespace, the Tags filter and sort, each tag page's filter, task status, and first row shown, the Tasks filter, rows shown, and capture draft, and folders made with New folder here. Typing and scrolling save after a 400 ms pause (`StoredWindow::remember`), a closing window writes anything still waiting, and each save writes its own temporary file before the rename so overlapping saves cannot interleave. Search reads open pages' unsaved text from a temporary FTS5 table filled with the overlay (`temp.overlay_search`) in place of their saved rows. Off macOS, Cmd/Ctrl-K, N, J, W, and \ are bound again for Vim page editors after Vim's keymap (`navigation::inside_pages`), so the notebook meaning wins over Vim's digraph, newline, window, and delete-word keys; an open completion menu keeps Ctrl-N. The frozen `tests/reference/daily/workspace.ts` is refreshed to rt `5ea5c22310ff`, and `tools/namespace-vectors.ts` writes 1696 `namespaceOf` cases that `quire_core` must match. Deviations: reading positions are source lines rather than rt's pixel offsets, so they survive window width and font changes; the Pages and Daily browsers share one remembered filter because they share one filter field; deleting a folder that holds nothing forgets it without a confirmation. Tests: `workspace_tests::reading_positions_filters_and_drafts_survive_reopening`, `file_tests::new_folder_here_shows_an_empty_folder_until_it_is_deleted`, `navigation_tests::notebook_ctrl_keys_win_over_vim_inside_a_page` (fails without the new bindings), `quire_core/tests/workspace.rs` (`view_choices_and_reading_positions_survive_save_load_rename_and_delete`, `new_folders_open_their_parents_survive_reload_and_go_with_their_parent`, `namespaces_match_the_reference_namespace_of_vectors`), and `quire_store/tests/index.rs` (`search_reads_unsaved_text_of_open_pages_instead_of_their_last_save`).

Batch L (2026-10-31, rt `5ea5c22310ff` README "includes empty folders and attachments"). A folder notebook's Files tree adds the folders and non-note files that `folder_notebook::list` finds, reread on the background executor with the page list (one read at a time, again if asked meanwhile); non-notes show a paperclip, open in the system app, and have Open, New note here, New folder here, and Reveal. Rename or move… on a folder with no page skips the preview: `FolderNotebook::move_noteless_folder` renames it on disk through the folder worker, refusing a taken name, a path inside itself, an invalid path, or a note that appeared there, and a Quire notebook moves its workspace folders (`Workspace::move_folder`). A folder notebook folder with files but no notes now goes through the delete dialog ("Delete Scans with its 1 file?") to the Trash instead of only being forgotten. Tests: `folder_tests::files_shows_a_folder_notebooks_empty_folders_and_attachments`, `folder_tests::renaming_a_folder_without_notes_moves_it_with_its_files`, `file_tests::renaming_a_quire_notebook_folder_without_pages_renames_it_here`, `folder_notebook::tests::a_folder_without_notes_moves_whole_unless_a_note_appeared_or_the_target_exists`, `workspace::a_moved_folder_takes_its_inner_folders_and_their_open_state_along`.

Batch M (2026-10-31, `e31a55aa7276`; rt `5ea5c22310ff` renames a moved folder through `VaultFiles.rename`). A folder that holds notes now moves whole. `quire_core::relocate::relocate_folder_pages` (with `FolderMove`) rewrites links as the folder rename implies: a link to any file inside the folder follows it, so a moved page's links to its neighbouring attachments stay as written and other pages' links to them follow; `QueryIndex::folder_link_candidates` adds pages whose text names the folder's last part (as written or with `%20`), so a page linking only to an attachment is rewritten too. The preview counts the folder's other files ("2 pages and 2 other files move."). A folder notebook applies it through `FolderNotebook::move_folder_if_heads` (`page_writes::move_folder`, `FolderClient::move_folder_if_heads`): every note checked against its base, every note on disk inside the folder must be among the edits (`Appeared`), the target must be free in any letter case, then a journal records a folder step (`Step::folder`, `folderTo`) ahead of each note's write in place, the folder is renamed with one `rename`, and the notes are written with their history carried from the old paths. A rename the disk refuses removes the journal and reports "Nothing was moved"; an interruption before or after the rename finishes on the next open, and Set aside or Retry see the folder step's state. A Quire notebook writes the pages at their new paths as before: its attachments are content-named in the store and resolve by name wherever pages go. The dialog also emits `FolderMoved`, so the workspace's folders and the Files listing follow. Deviation: notes of a moved folder are no longer archived in `retired/`; their replaced texts are in saved history. Tests: `quire_core/tests/relocate.rs::a_folder_moving_whole_carries_links_to_the_files_inside_it`, `folder_notebook::folder_move_tests` (6: whole move with attachments, scans, and an empty folder; refusals for a taken name in either case, inside itself, a stale note, a path off the folder, a note that appeared, each with nothing moved; a rename refused by a read-only parent with no journal left; interruption before the rename and after it at each of four steps, each finished on reopen; the noteless move), `folder_tests::moving_a_folder_moves_it_whole_with_its_attachments_and_keeps_tabs` (a page linking only to the folder's image and PDF is rewritten), and `move_tests::renaming_a_folder_moves_every_page_inside_and_rewrites_links_to_them` (a Quire notebook page's `attachments/` link stays as written).
Remaining: files and folders added outside Quire appear only after the next page-list refresh or a Refresh, as in `rt`; profile switches (NR33); a reading position written within 400 ms of quitting through the quit guard rather than a window close is lost (closing a window writes it).
Batch F: the sidebar is a GPUI tab group (`chrome.rs`, `crate::tab_order::TabOrder` for indices): Tab and Shift-Tab (`NextControl`, `PreviousControl` in the `QuireSidebar` context) walk the ten buttons, the Templates entry, favorites, recents and their remove buttons, and the Files tree, whose focus handle is the last stop; a focused entry opens with Enter or Space as a click does, Shift-Enter beside. Test: `template_tests::the_keyboard_reaches_the_templates_entry_and_the_guides_add_buttons` (Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-E, Shift-Tab back to the Templates entry, Enter creating `Templates.md`).

Batch J (window chrome, rt `5ea5c22310ff`; `App.tsx` 1146-1201, 1286-1292, 1405-1467, `NotebookBrowser.tsx` 214-222). Tabs carry an icon by destination (page, tag, Tags, Tasks, Daily notes, Pages) and an amber dot while their page saves or a red one when it needs attention, with the reason in the tab tooltip (`chrome.rs` `tab_icon`, `tab_save_state`). The sidebar toggle (`quire_window::ToggleSidebar`, toolbar and View > Sidebar; rt has no key, and Command-B is Bold here) hides the sidebar and is kept in the workspace's new `chrome` group (`quire_core::workspace::ChromeChoices`), as are a collapsed FILES heading and the Daily notes month. The FILES heading is a disclosure button with Refresh files beside it (`refresh_files`: a folder notebook rereads its folder, then the page list is read again); both are tab stops before the tree, and a collapsed tree leaves the Tab order. Daily notes has a month menu (Zed `DropdownMenu`: All months, then each month with daily pages and their count, newest first) in place of rt's free month input, since only months with pages can match. Tests: `page_views_tests` (`the_save_icon_and_tab_dot_follow_saving`, `the_vim_button_and_sidebar_toggle_change_what_they_name`, `daily_notes_show_one_remembered_month`, `the_files_heading_collapses_and_refresh_lists_new_files`), `quire_core/tests/workspace.rs` `chrome_choices_survive_save_and_load_and_stay_out_of_older_files`. Remaining for batch J here: Refresh is not proven to beat the file watcher (the folder test passes with either). Batch M (`3d6e61c88046`): migration carries rt's `filesOpen: false` into `chrome.files_collapsed` and `browsers.daily.month` (only `YYYY-MM` with a real month) into `chrome.daily_month` (`rt_migration::tests::migration_imports_tabs_favorites_and_prompt_history_once_and_reads_rt_only` with both set, and malformed values ignored in `a_renamed_page_and_unknown_destinations_are_dropped_from_converted_tabs`); rt's `ai` tab now migrates as the Ask the notebook tab (NR30).

Batch K (rt `5ea5c22310ff`). Find lists rt's commands above the pages (`App.tsx` 1690-1770): Appearance: Light, Dark, and Follow system once the query has text, dispatching `UseLightAppearance`, `UseDarkAppearance`, and `UseSystemAppearance`, and the AI entries (NR30); the new page prompt and the block-move palette offer none. Find also lists up to eight blocks per page whose text holds the query anywhere, ignoring case (`App.tsx` 2511-2525), from `QueryIndex::text_hits` on the index thread over `live_blocks`, so unsaved text counts; SQLite folds only ASCII case, so a query with other cased letters narrows by its longest ASCII or caseless run and is checked again in Rust. Hits open at their block through `Jump::at`, IDs or not. Keys (`Editor.tsx` 2296-2305): Mod-E switches Write and Source with Command-Shift-M (Ctrl-Shift-M) kept as an alias, Mod-Y redoes, and Mod-Shift-C copies the block reference at the caret. Off macOS, Ctrl-E and Ctrl-Y are Vim's (scroll in Normal, insert from below and above in Insert), so they switch and redo only with Vim off (`!vim_mode`); Quire's own key bindings take no Zed default for Command-E (`buffer_search::UseSelectionForFind`), so no conflict on macOS. Mod-Backspace deletes to the text start on every platform, as in rt (Ctrl-Backspace off macOS, batch I). Tests: `quire_store/tests/index.rs::text_hits_find_substrings_in_unsaved_text_up_to_eight_per_page`, `navigation::palette::tests` (commands by AI, Vim, and page; ordering and when-typed), `navigation_tests::find_palette_lists_blocks_holding_the_typed_text_unsaved_text_included`, `page_menu_tests::reference_editor_keys_switch_views_copy_a_block_reference_and_redo`, and, off macOS, `page_menu_tests::vim_keeps_ctrl_e_and_ctrl_y_off_macos`. Commits `a6cae47b8b3f`, `b6460a1ae4bd`, `76b61c83b2ad`.
Status: in_progress

#### NR16.1: Port daily date and layout rules for indexed opening

What to do: Port daily formatting, configured-title parsing, ISO/weekday/Roam date recognition, layout validation/detection, and notebook settings into the shared core. Freeze the original daily/workspace helpers and compare native results with generated cases before wiring indexed page selection.
Constraints: Keep layout state notebook-scoped. Preserve invalid settings and unknown versions without overwriting them. Validate native formatting dates explicitly. Keep source text and paths unchanged. Do not replace title-based daily selection with a path-only lookup, or load every page body during opening. Finish fixture regeneration and formatting before starting Cargo verification gates.
Write set: core daily helpers/tests, frozen oracle provenance, deterministic fixture generator, task proof and README.
Verify: Differential valid dates, leap/month/year boundaries, incorrect weekdays, configured patterns, duplicate tokens, Unicode literals, layout detection and ties, ISO/Roam recognition, malformed/unknown settings, and unchanged original source hashes. Test injected local-calendar normalization, including skipped days, before declaring date parity. Indexed storage selection and native navigation remain NR12/NR16 requirements.
Proof notes: Added the civil/UTC foundation in `quire_core::daily`: formatting, configured pattern parsing, ISO/weekday/Roam recognition, layout validation/detection, and settings serialization/validation. `DailyParser` retains its compiled configuration per notebook instead of using the source's process-global active layout. Recognition retains the source's early four-digit-year check. The parser handles JavaScript's numeric-constructor years 0..99, Roam month prefixes/year interpretation and day overflow, greedy duplicate tokens, literal escaping, and non-Unicode regex case canonicalization. Layout detection preserves first-seen ties and single boundary-delimited month replacement.

Native formatting requires a valid four-digit civil ISO date and returns an actionable error for invalid dates rather than producing a path. Settings parse through the shared lossless JSON implementation. Malformed/unknown-version settings remain read-only. Unpaired-surrogate layout strings produce an explicit read-only native representation error, retaining the original caller-owned source; this remains the existing native display limitation, not complete UTF-16 layout parity. No settings file or notebook is written by these helpers.

Copied unchanged daily-layout/workspace modules from the frozen e91f3e23603c76a1b1d824ec3dd64c60096e5d01 source and recorded/verified their hashes. `TZ=UTC bun tools/daily-vectors.ts` generates 4,831 cases. A Node-target bundle executed by Node emits identical fixture bytes. Four native tests compare that corpus, isolate two notebook parsers, reject invalid dates as paths, preserve unrepresentable settings, and exercise short Unicode filenames. The Unicode regression first reproduced a byte-boundary panic (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-unicode-red.log`); detection now reuses the canonical page-name helper instead of slicing filenames itself.

`cargo test -p quire -p quire_core -p quire_store --locked --features quire/test-support` passes 280 tests (145 app, 24 core, 111 storage; `/private/tmp/quire-daily-all-tests.log`). Core all-target Clippy with warnings denied passes (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-clippy.log`). The locked normal native build passes with existing dependency artifacts (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-build.log`), retaining its existing linker warning and previously recorded clean Metal-build limitation. Formatting, prose lint, and scoped whitespace checks pass. The lockfile adds only a core edge to the existing chrono version; no package version, schema, vendor source, or app workflow changes.

Sequential review checked source provenance, parser scoping and compilation reuse, JavaScript date/year quirks, regex literal/case boundaries, Unicode filenames, stable detection ties, settings truthiness/version/error handling, original-source preservation, and explicit formatting preconditions. Initial review exposed that formatting 2011-12-30 with a weekday yields Friday in UTC and Saturday in Pacific/Apia, whose calendar skipped that day. The civil-only foundation therefore remained open until the timezone work below.

`DailyCalendar` now supplies an explicit timezone to formatting, parsing, detection, and each retained `DailyParser`. Its injected Unix-millisecond clock computes local dates without reading the process clock. Named/system timezone discovery returns actionable errors and belongs in background startup; retained calendars perform date operations without rereading ambient timezone settings. Replaced the initial chrono edge with the already locked Jiff 0.2.15 implementation for civil arithmetic and IANA gap/fold data. The only lockfile difference replaces that one core dependency edge; no package version changes.

The adapter uses JavaScript-compatible local normalization: advance through gaps and retain the original civil date in folds. Numeric constructors use noon and the source's 0..99 year remapping; ISO title checks use literal years; Roam date strings use midnight. It inspects ambiguous offsets directly rather than converting every civil date through a timestamp, retaining valid four-digit dates at 9999-12-31 beyond Jiff's narrower timestamp upper bound. Unsupported injected instants return errors rather than a wrong date or implicit UTC fallback.

`bun tools/daily-timezone-vectors.ts` bundles the unchanged frozen helpers and an exact two-line extraction of localDate from the pinned tasks module, then launches eight separate Node processes with explicit TZ values. It emits 784 date/layout/clock cases across UTC, Riyadh, New York, Lord Howe, Apia, Kwajalein, Sao Paulo, and Paris. Cases cover skipped days, a repeated historical day, DST boundaries, injected instants crossing midnight, configured/ISO/Roam matching, layout detection, and the first/last four-digit years. Node 26.11.1 and Bun 1.4.2 are recorded separately from the original protocol snapshot runtime. Repeated generation emits identical bytes; the localDate extraction matches its pinned source and recorded hash exactly.

All 282 app/core/storage tests pass (145 app, 26 core, 111 storage; `/private/tmp/quire-daily-timezone-all-tests.log`). The initial focused compile failed for the absent calendar API (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-timezone-red.log`); the original 4,831 cases and new timezone cases now pass together. Core all-target Clippy with warnings denied passes (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-timezone-clippy.log`). The locked normal native build passes with existing dependency artifacts (`/private/tmp/quire-daily-timezone-build.log`), retaining the recorded linker/clean-Metal-build limitations. Formatting, prose lint, source-extraction/hash review, deterministic fixture generation, and scoped whitespace checks pass.

Final sequential review checked explicit timezone ownership, no ambient clock reads, compatible gap/fold normalization, numeric versus string constructor rules, midnight versus noon, maximum-year arithmetic, discovery/error boundaries, parser isolation, timestamp errors, and unchanged source/settings preservation. The simplification pass uses one existing date library for both civil arithmetic and timezone rules and adds no JavaScript runtime to the app. This completes the shared helper and injected-calendar slice. Indexed title lookup, guarded page creation, native daily navigation, unsupported native UTF-16 layout display, and full NR16 acceptance remain open.
Status: done

#### NR16.2: Port pointer menus and in-place rename

What to do: Give Files, Recent, tabs, and the page one shared pointer menu, also opened with Shift-F10. File items offer Open, Open beside, Copy link, favorites, New note here, New folder here, Rename, Move to, Reveal in Finder or Explorer, and Delete page; Recent adds Remove from recent. Rename, also F2, edits the name in place: Enter or clicking away applies and Escape cancels. The page menu offers Cut, Copy, Paste, Select all, toggled inline marks on a selection (Bold with Command/Ctrl-B, Italic as `_`, Strikethrough, Code, Link to page), Bullet or Numbered list with the current style ticked, task actions, Copy block reference or embed, and Ask AI. A right-click outside the selection moves the caret first.
Constraints: Menu actions call the commands owned by NR14, NR18, NR25, and NR28 rather than duplicating them. Mark toggling matches `toggleMark` in `src/editor/page-menu.ts` and applies as one undo step. Keyboard access and accessible names are required.
Dependencies: NR14, NR18, NR25, NR28.
Verify: Port `rt/tests/page-menu.test.ts`, the menu cases in `file-actions` and `tab-menu`, and the e2e editor menu scenarios to native pointer and keyboard tests.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` `35e673677291`, checked against 0.9.57 `5ea5c22` (`src/ui/App.tsx` `fileMenuItems` and `tabMenuItems`, `src/ui/ContextMenu.tsx`, `src/ui/PageList.tsx`, `src/editor/Editor.tsx` `pageMenu`, `src/editor/page-menu.ts`). Commits `f19d5a2a768c` (Files, Favorites, Recent, tabs) and `ee96945a37dd` (page). Every menu is a Zed `ui::ContextMenu` described as data in `navigation/pointer_menu.rs` (items, separators trimmed as rt trims them, disabled and ticked entries) and built per open. Files menus highlight the row and dispatch the Files tree's own actions from its focus, as Zed's project panel does, so the menu and the keys share one handler; tab and sidebar menus run the method behind the action on the clicked item and show that action's keys, as Zed's tab menu does; the page menu is each page editor's `set_custom_context_menu` and dispatches editor actions from the editor. Shift-F10 opens the highlighted Files item's menu, the page menu (Zed's `editor::OpenContextMenu`), or elsewhere the active tab's menu. The `menu` key context gets Up, Down, Home, End, Ctrl-P, Ctrl-N, Enter, Space, Escape, and Tab (closes, as rt does); Escape returns focus to what opened the menu.

Inventory (rt item: quire action, status). Files page (`Page actions for <title>`): Open: `menu::Confirm`; Open beside: `menu::SecondaryConfirm`; Copy link (`[[title]]`): new `CopyLink`; Add to or Remove from favorites: `ToggleFavorite` (now also handled in Files for the highlighted page); New note here: new `NewPageHere` (the create palette names the folder); New folder here: remaining, quire keeps no empty-folder inventory; Rename: `RenameFile` (F2); Move to…: `MoveFile` (Shift-F2); Reveal in Finder / Explorer: new `RevealInFileManager`, folder notebooks only, as rt shows it only with desktop files; Delete page…: `DeleteFile`. Files folder (`Folder actions for <path>`): New note here: `NewPageHere`; New folder here: remaining; Rename or move…: `RenameFile` (quire addition, rt has no folder rename); Reveal: `RevealInFileManager`; Delete folder…: `DeleteFile`. Recent (`Page actions for <title>`): Open, Open beside, Copy link, favorites, Rename or move… (`ask_move`, keys of `MovePage`), Reveal, Remove from recent (the Recent close button's method), Delete page… (`ask_delete`, keys of `DeletePage`). Favorites: the Recent menu without Remove from recent (quire addition; rt's favorites have no menu). Tabs (`Tab actions for <label>`): Close (keys of `CloseTab`), Close others, Close tabs to the left, Close tabs to the right (each disabled as rt disables it, showing the clicked tab when the active one closes), Close all, favorites, then for page tabs Open beside, Copy link, Reveal, Rename or move…, Delete page…. Page (`Page actions`): Cut, Copy, Paste, Select all (Zed editor actions; Cut and Copy disabled without a selection, Cut and Paste on a read-only page); on an editable selection Bold (new `ToggleBold`, Command-B or Ctrl-B outside Vim Normal), Italic (`_`), Strikethrough, Code, Link to page (new `ToggleItalic`, `ToggleStrikethrough`, `ToggleCode`, `ToggleLinkToPage`, porting `toggleMark` as one undo step); in a list item Bullet list and Numbered list ticked by the current style (`BulletList`, `NumberList`), Turn into task, Mark task done, or Reopen task (`ToggleTask`); Copy block reference and Copy block embed (new `CopyBlockReference`, `CopyBlockEmbed`, giving the item an ID when it has none); Ask AI…: remaining, quire has no AI. A right-click outside the selection moves the caret first.

Tests: `pointer_menu::tests` (separator trimming and the text form), `page_menu::tests` (the three `page-menu.test.ts` `toggleMark` cases plus empty pairs at the text start and between identical marks), `menu_tests.rs` (Shift-F10 on a Files page and folder with exact names and items and Escape returning focus to Files; a simulated right-click on Files rows choosing Copy link, favorites, Rename and Move opening the dialogs F2 and Shift-F2 open, Delete page…, and Open; New note here creating `Projects/Fresh.md`; the `tab-menu.test.tsx` cases: closing to the right and left with the menu's tab shown, all three close items disabled on a lone tab, Add to favorites, Close all leaving Pages, and a browser tab without page actions; Shift-F10 outside Files opening the active tab's menu; Recent and Favorites menus removing from recent, opening, and deleting), `folder_tests::renaming_a_folder_page_from_the_files_menu_writes_what_f2_writes` (the e2e "renames a page in place from the Files menu" started from a right-click: the exact menu with Reveal, the same bytes written as F2's test, and Reveal reaching the stand-in file manager with the file's path), and `page_menu_tests.rs` (the e2e "formats and restyles blocks from the page's right-click menu" in Write: exact items, Numbered list, Turn into task, Bold keeping the word selected and undone in one step, Escape returning focus to the page; in Source the list styles are absent, Copy block reference writes ` ^id` and copies `((id))`, Copy block embed copies `{{embed: ((id))}}` with the same ID, and a right-click below the text moves the caret). Choosing goes through the keyboard (Down past disabled items, then Enter), so each test also covers menu navigation; removing the menu Escape binding fails two tests. Items are chosen with the keyboard because GPUI's `ContextMenu` exposes no entry list; the menus' text form comes from the data that builds them.

Deviations: Rename from Files opens the rename dialog, as F2 already does (NR25.2), rather than editing in place. Menus are labelled for assistive technology by a group around the Files, sidebar, and tab menus; the page menu has no name because Zed's editor renders it. List styles appear in Write only, where quire's list commands work. Paste from the menu inserts the clipboard as Zed's Paste does (as rt's menu replaces the selection); Write's block-splitting paste stays on Command-V. Shift-F10 outside Files opens the active tab's menu at the pointer, since tabs take no focus. Remaining: New note or folder here for empty folders, Ask AI… (no AI feature), and the keyboard menu key (GPUI has no key name for it).
Status: done

### NR17: Port tag pages and linked references

What to do: Add normalized tag completion/index, grouped context, filters, reference ordering, task filters, and source navigation.
Constraints: Viewing tags never assigns IDs; retained scroll/filter state uses bounded results.
Write set: tag and linked-reference views, query contract tests.
Dependencies: NR12, NR15, NR16.
Verify: Port tag/tag-page/linked-reference scenarios; check unsaved edits, nested/Unicode tags, ancestor context, ordering, and rapid filter changes without stale results.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` 0.9.57 (`5ea5c22`), `src/ui/NotebookBrowser.tsx` (Tags view), `src/ui/TagPage.tsx`, `src/ui/TagEdits.tsx`, `src/ui/LinkedReferences.tsx`, and `src/core/workspace.ts` (`tag` destinations, `oldestFirst`).

The views are GPUI entities in `crates/quire/src/navigation/`: `tags.rs` (`TagsView`), `tag_edits.rs` (`TagEdits`, a child entity of the Tags view), `tag_page.rs` (`TagPageView`), and `references.rs` (`ReferencesView`), with `context_rows.rs` laying out a block with its parents and nested blocks for both the tag page and the references panel. The stored window wires them in `editor_host/stored/tag_views.rs` and `reference_views.rs`. `quire_core::workspace` gains `BrowserView::Tags`, `Destination::Tag { tag }` (saved, restored, and shown in tabs, recents, favorites, and history like the reference's `tag:` key), and `oldest_first` per page. `IndexSync` now emits `IndexChanged` when an analysis step moves the index generation (saves analyzed, pages removed) and when an open page's overlay is set or released; the window re-runs the queries on screen in response. `navigation::live::Live` holds each view's result with a request sequence number and the generation from `with_generation`; a reply applies only to the latest request and never replaces a newer generation, so rapid filter edits and slow replies cannot show stale rows. Each view's query runs in one worker step: the tag page reads `tagged_blocks` and `outlines` together, and the references panel reads `linked_references`, `filter_names`, the filtered set, and `reference_facets` together, so rows, count, and chips always describe one filter and one index state. `quire_store::index::Outline` (nearest four parents, first eight nested blocks with depth, the rest counted) comes from the same page rows the filter contexts read; `QueryIndex::outlines` serves the tag page and `ReferenceBlock::outline` the panel. `QueryIndex::date` became public for the panel's order toggle.

Keys: the Tags button or the `ShowTags` action opens the Tags browser; arrows, Home, End, Enter open a tag page; Command/Ctrl-Enter toggles selection. Tag pages and the references panel use arrows, Enter (open the source page), and Shift-Enter or Shift-click (open beside). Command/Ctrl-Shift-R focuses the references panel; Escape in its filter clears it.

Deviations from `rt`, chosen deliberately. Lists are virtualized `uniform_list`s instead of "Show more" pages of 40 or 60; the references panel computes chips from the first 40 references the filter keeps, which equals the reference's first page. Rows are single lines: blocks show their indexed snippet as plain text rather than rendered rich text with clickable links and tags, parents show as one breadcrumb line, and more than eight nested blocks show a count instead of an expand button. Opening a source opens the page at its top because native block reveal is not built (NR16 remaining). Tag pages and reference panels are cached per tag and per page for the session, which keeps each one's filter as the reference does, but filter text, tag sort, and scroll are not written to the workspace file. The references panel sits below the editor with a fixed height and a collapse button rather than at the end of the scrolled page.

Tests: `editor_host/stored/tags_tests.rs` (tag list counts, labels, case-insensitive filter, keyboard open of a tag page; tag page grouping, parents and nested blocks, refresh after another writer saves, filter, Shift-Enter beside and Enter; sorting; rename preview of unsaved typing and nested tags then apply to an open buffer and a closed page with selection cleared and the list refreshed; invalid name, typing clearing the error, stale preview refused with nothing written, empty selection; twenty changes then show more), `references_tests.rs` (the port of rt `linked-references-filter.test.tsx`: typed word matching a nested block, Escape, Alt-click page chip giving `-[[Launch]]`, no-match state, unclosed quote shown and ignored; daily order newest then oldest kept in the workspace, a new referring page appearing after a save, Shift-Enter and Enter), `navigation::live` (superseded requests and older generations rejected), `context_rows` and `tag_page` unit tests, `quire_core/tests/reference_filter.rs` (the rt `toggleTerm` cases plus `term_state`), and `quire_core/tests/workspace.rs` (tag destinations and `oldest_first` survive save, load, and missing-page pruning).

Tag completion and tag clicks (batch B). Typing `#` and a partial tag in a page editor, outside code, opens Zed's completion menu with the notebook's tags from `tag_names`, fuzzy-matched by Zed and leaving out the exact spelling being typed; accepting replaces the whole partial tag (`references/completion.rs::tag_completions`, which waits for the page's parse so the tag range comes from the current inline analysis). In Write with Format tags on, each painted tag is a text region (`tag_labels.rs::TagLinks`) that opens its tag page through the new `Notebook::show_tag` on click, unless the caret of the focused editor is in the tag's block, where a click places the caret to edit the tag. Tests: `completion_tests.rs::typing_a_tag_offers_the_notebooks_tags_outside_code_and_completes_the_whole_tag` (menu entries, Enter and Tab, nothing inside inline code) and `rendered_links_tests.rs::clicking_a_tag_in_write_opens_its_tag_page_unless_the_caret_is_in_its_block`.

Batch C. Opening a tag or reference row reveals its block (NR16 notes). Tag pages whose blocks include tasks offer All, Open, Due, and Done with counts, as rt `TagPage` does: `QueryIndex::tagged_tasks` pairs each tagged block with the task whose block starts there, the status is kept per tag in the workspace with the filter and first row shown, and the Tags browser keeps its filter and sort. Linked tasks sit above linked references (NR19 notes). Tests: `tags_tests::tag_pages_filter_tasks_by_status_and_remember_it`, `quire_store/tests/index.rs::tagged_tasks_pair_each_tagged_task_block_with_its_task`, and the reopen test in NR16.

Batch F. Rows draw block text through one shared renderer, `crates/quire/src/rich_text.rs`, which replaces the plain-snippet deviation above for tag pages, linked references, Tasks, linked tasks, live task lists, and search hits. `rich_text::analyze` parses the text with the vendored inline grammar the page editor highlights with (`tree_sitter_quire_markdown::INLINE_LANGUAGE`), finds references and tags with `quire_core::markdown::references` and `tags` given the code and link ranges the grammar marks (the index's own rules), drops task markers (`read_marker`) and Markdown delimiters, and returns the shown text with marks (strong, emphasis, strike, code, link, tag tone, faint) and link targets; `rich_text::line` draws it as one GPUI `StyledText` line with `TextRun`s (buffer font for code, tag tone ink and background from `quire_core::tag_tones`), truncated, inside an `InteractiveText` whose link clicks stop the row's own click. Page and block links open through the window's `follow` (Shift beside), tags open their tag page, a Markdown link without a scheme names a page by path, and web addresses open in the browser; `RowLink::open` does the same through a page editor's `Notebook` for live task lists. A per-view `RichCache` parses each text once. Page groups fold with the `ListItem` disclosure or Left and Right on a row (`context_rows::Expansion`, kept per view for the session); a "N more nested blocks" row loads 50 more in place on Enter or click through the new `QueryIndex::outlines_with` (an outline with a per-block limit; `outlines` delegates to it). Escape in a tag page or empty reference filter moves focus to the rows and `/` returns (`FocusRowFilter`). The side pane now draws its own page's linked tasks and references under its editor (`reference_views.rs::side_references`), built and refreshed with the main pane's; opening a source from it replaces the side pane's page and Shift opens it in the main pane, mirroring the main panel, as each rt `PagePane` carries its own references. Deviations: a block's loaded nested blocks are keyed by its page revision and position, so they fold back to eight when that page is saved; folded groups are not written to the workspace. Tests: `rich_text` unit tests (markup hidden, labels and targets, tones outside code and links, relative Markdown links, cache), `context_rows` unit tests (folding, activation, longer outlines), `quire_store/tests/index.rs::outlines_list_as_many_nested_blocks_as_asked_and_count_the_rest`, `tags_tests::tag_page_groups_fold_load_nested_blocks_in_place_and_row_tags_open` (Escape, ten Downs and Enter loading two more children, Left and Right, `/`, and a click on a row's `#other` opening its tag page), and `references_tests::the_side_pane_shows_its_own_pages_linked_references` (Enter replacing the side page and revealing the block, Shift-Enter opening in the main pane).
Remaining: none.
Batch K (rt `5ea5c22310ff`, `TagPage.tsx` 143-157). With AI commands on and at least one tagged block, Synthesize beside the block count opens the answer pane titled Blocks tagged #tag over every block carrying the tag, unfiltered as in rt, through the linked references synthesis path (NR30). Test: `answer_tests::synthesize_on_a_tag_page_sends_every_block_carrying_the_tag`. Commit `ca2bdfbd5d85`.
Status: done

#### NR17.1: Filter linked references

What to do: Add the reference filter line with `#tag`, `[[Page]]`, `project:Name`, and word terms, each negatable with `-`. A reference matches when every term holds for its context: the referring block, its ancestors, its children, and its page, whose title counts as a link and whose front matter tags count as tags. Show chips for the most frequent tags, pages, and projects (click adds, Alt-click excludes) and an "N of M" count. Keep the filter per page for the session.
Constraints: Evaluate over bounded indexed results with the live overlay; rapid filter edits cannot show stale results.
Verify: Every case in `rt/tests/fixtures/reference-filters.json`, which the Go terminal app also runs, plus ports of the `reference-filter` and `linked-references-filter` tests.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` `42de3c8949e1` (`src/core/reference-filter.ts`). Logic and data are in place; the view is not. `quire_core::reference_filter` ports `queryTokens` (with JavaScript whitespace), `parseReferenceFilter` (UTF-16 length rules), and `referenceMatcher`. `quire_store::index::references` ports `referenceBlocks`, `orderReferences`, `referenceContexts`, `projectPages`, page and project name resolution, and `referenceFacets` over the live views, so unsaved edits count. Index format 3 and analyzer version 2 add each block's raw content (contexts read IDs as the reference does) and overlay front tags. `reference_filter_tests::linked_reference_filters_match_the_shared_fixture` runs all 17 shared cases through the production analyzer and index and checks facets; `crates/quire_core/tests/reference_filter.rs` covers token splitting on JavaScript whitespace, edge terms, the unclosed quote, and nested tag matching. The shared fixture exposed a duplicate front-tag insert in index writes, now fixed. The view is now in (NR17 proof notes): `navigation::references::ReferencesView` under the main page shows the filter line once there are two references, the parse error inline (an unreadable filter hides nothing, as in the reference), an "N of M" count, a clear button, Escape to clear, and up to eight chips from `reference_facets` (click adds the term, Alt-click adds `-term`, a second click removes it; `quire_core::reference_filter::toggle_term` and `term_state` port `toggleTerm` and the chip state). Each page's panel lives for the session, so its filter does too. References, names, the filtered set, and chips come from one `with_generation` step, and `Live` drops superseded requests and older generations. `references_tests::linked_references_filter_by_typed_terms_and_chips` ports the rt `linked-references-filter.test.tsx` scenario with keystrokes; the `toggleTerm` cases are in `crates/quire_core/tests/reference_filter.rs`.
Status: done

#### NR17.2: Rename and delete tags in bulk

What to do: Let Tags select several tags. Rename moves each chosen tag, and the tags nested under it, to a new name, merging into an existing tag; Delete removes only the chosen tags. Both rewrite blocks and front matter `tags`, preview every changed page, and apply as one change of at most 1,000 pages.
Constraints: Leave tags inside code and links unchanged, preserve spacing and the three front matter forms, reject invalid names, and refuse a stale preview. Apply through the shared atomic multi-page edit path on both backends.
Verify: Port `rt/tests/tag-edits.test.ts` and the notebook-browser rename case; test the 1,000-page bound and stale-preview refusal.
Proof notes: Planning is done; applying is not. `quire_core::tag_edits` ports `retag`, the per-page rewrite with the deleted-tag spacing rule, the three front matter forms, name validation, and the 1,000-page refusal. Callers pass tag tokens from the editor analyzer (`page_index::edit_tags`), so tags in code and links stay without a second parser. Unlike rt, which plans over every page, the index picks candidates (`tag_edit_pages`) from block and front matter tag keys. Tests port the rt rename, delete, front matter, and refusal cases (`page_index::tag_edit_tests`), check candidate selection end to end (`query_index::tag_edit_tests`), and cover nested renames and the limit (`quire_core/tests/tag_edits.rs`). Edits come back as `SourceEdit`s on normalized text, so writing them through `SourceText::apply` keeps every line break the page had, where rt rewrites a CRLF page entirely in CRLF. The store side of applying is in: `save_many_if_heads` checks every page's heads in the save transaction and writes all pages or none. `tag_edit::plan` reads open pages from their live buffers (unsaved typing included) and closed pages from their stored heads; `tag_edit::apply` refuses if any open buffer changed since the preview, saves each open page first and holds its further saves, waits until no save or merge is in flight, writes every page through `save_many_if_heads` with each open page's current base, then hands open pages their new revision through `absorb`. Saving open pages first matters: without it the absorb merge sees the typed lines twice and duplicates them (`tag_edit::tests` fails that way). Tests cover a mixed open and closed rename keeping unsaved typing, and stale-preview refusal from typing and from a closed page saved after the preview. Source: `rt` `957da33d4766` (`src/core/tag-edits.ts`).

The Tags selection and preview UI is in, so `tag_edit`, `page_index::edit_tags`, and the `PagePersistence` coordination methods (`buffer`, `base`, `path`, `hold`, `is_idle`, `release`) now compile in production with their test-only gates removed. In the Tags browser a checkbox or Command/Ctrl-Enter selects tags; `navigation::tag_edits::TagEdits` takes a name (Enter or Rename… previews a rename, Delete… previews a delete), lists every change by page twenty at a time with "Show more changes", and applies through `tag_edit::apply`. Typing a name or changing the selection drops the preview and any planning in flight; an apply already started always finishes so open pages are never left held. Errors show inline: `INVALID_NAME`, `NOTHING_CHOSEN`, the 1,000-page refusal from planning, and `STALE_PREVIEW`. After a successful apply the selection and name clear and the list re-queries. The preview heading matches the rt notebook-browser rename case ("Rename #books, #reading to #library on 1 page?"). Tests: `tags_tests::renaming_selected_tags_previews_every_change_then_rewrites_open_and_closed_pages`, `invalid_names_empty_selections_and_stale_previews_show_inline_errors`, and `long_previews_list_twenty_changes_until_asked_for_more`. The 1,000-page bound stays tested in `quire_core/tests/tag_edits.rs`; the window shows that refusal through the same inline error path as the others.
Status: done

### NR18: Port task semantics and dates

What to do: Implement checkbox/Roam task parsing, state transitions, capture, created/due/completed stamps, inherited dates/project links, and batch edits.
Constraints: Task operations edit Markdown through shared buffers and preserve IDs. Date behavior is deterministic under an injected clock/timezone.
Write set: `quire_core` tasks/projects/date modules and fixtures.
Dependencies: NR10, NR12, NR14.
Verify: Differential task/date/project tests cover state reopening, code exclusions, daily capture, stamps, inherited links, multiple selections, and undo.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` `5ea5c22310ff` (`src/core/tasks.ts` frozen unchanged as `tests/reference/core/tasks.ts`; the task methods of `src/core/store.ts` extracted as pure planners in `tests/reference/core/task-commands.ts`; both recorded in `tests/reference/UPSTREAM.json`). `quire_core::tasks` ports marker reading, `tasksInPage` (states from state tags and Roam `#[[state]]` tags outside code and links, created/due/completed stamps, due from own then inherited date links then the daily page, inherited page links, shown text), `taskChanges` (toggle, set state, reopen, completion stamp), `taskDateChange` with `setTaskDate` validation, `taskAt`, `tasksIn`, `captureTask`, the created stamp (none on today's daily page), `addDays`, `isDue`, `isUpcoming`, `shortDate`, `taskDate`, `taskFilter`, and `projectMembership`. Callers pass blocks, tags, references, tag-exclusion ranges, and code-block ranges from native analysis, today's ISO date from `DailyCalendar::date_at` over an injected clock, and a `DailyNames` for the daily layout; the core never reads the process clock or parses Markdown. `quire::tasks` adds `ToggleTask` (Cmd-Enter, Ctrl-Enter elsewhere) and `CycleTask` (Cmd-Shift-Enter) in every page editor, Write and Source, Vim on or off, including embedded excerpts (offsets map through excerpt anchors). A caret runs `task_at`, a selection runs `tasks_in`; each applies one `Editor::transact` with a structural edit through the shared buffer and places the caret after a new checkbox. The Write checkbox now toggles through the same command, so completing stamps the date. Refusals use the outline notice. `TaskContext` (a global) holds the clock, time zone, daily layout, and a page-title lookup. Tests: `tools/task-vectors.ts` generates 224 pages (48 handwritten from rt `tasks.test.ts` plus marker, stamp, inherited-date, Roam, Unicode, quote, code, and table edges, and 120 seeded) under two daily layouts, with every state change, date change, `taskAt` at three positions per line, `tasksIn` over line ranges, 352 captures, date helpers, and filters; generation is byte-identical in UTC and America/New_York. `quire_core/tests/tasks.rs` runs them over the reference analysis; `tasks::tests::native_analysis_matches_the_frozen_task_vectors` runs them over the native tree-sitter analysis. `tests/reference/tasks.test.ts` (Vitest) reruns rt's own Notebook expectations through the extraction. GPUI tests drive real keys with exact before/after text: making a task with the caret kept and the ID preserved (Write and Source, Vim on and off), the full cycle and toggle sequence with a single undo per step, Roam reopening that drops `#done`, multi-line selections including Vim `V j`, refusals in code, headings, and duplicate IDs, no stamp on today's daily page, and other editors keeping their own Cmd-Enter. Deviations from `rt`: offsets are bytes and a task carries its block key and start instead of a `path:key:from` string; `taskChanges` reads the task's tags from the page analysis instead of reparsing the raw text (every vector agrees); rt caches a block's links by block key, so a second block with the same ID reads the first one's links, and Quire reads each block's own (vectors adjusted, regression test `a_repeated_block_id_reads_its_own_links_not_the_first_blocks`). The Write caret clamp moved an unmoved caret in the checkbox gap onto the checkbox on any re-clamp; it now keeps it, so the caret stays after a new empty task's checkbox (covered by `shortcut_makes_a_task_at_the_caret_and_keeps_the_id_and_caret`). Eight seeded pages, each ending in an unclosed fence line with no final newline right after a list item, parse into different blocks under the tree-sitter grammar than under the reference parser; the native test lists them and checks only that blocks differ there. Commands: both scoped Clippy gates with `-D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`, the full `cargo test -p quire -p quire_core -p quire_store` gate (216 quire tests), and the reference Vitest run (90 tests in 4 files) pass. Multiple carets (commit `6d7901cc1cd5`): `tasks::run` plans each caret and selection on the newest selection's page and `combine` applies their edits once each in one undo step, skipping edits that overlap an earlier caret's, so two carets on one task change it once; only a lone caret is placed, and the command fails only when every caret's plan does (`task_commands_act_at_every_caret_in_one_undo_step`). Deviation: rt acts on its main selection only.
Remaining: capture into today's daily page (the reference `captureTarget: "today"` preference; Inbox capture is in, see NR19). Closed by the S1 batch: the grammar divergence above (a fence line ending at EOF and a fence closing after text on a line; see NR37); every task vector now matches the reference blocks. Closed by NR19: task rows in the query index with date sources (`Page::undated_tasks` and `DateSources`), `TaskContext` wired to the notebook's clock, time zone, daily layout, and page titles in the stored window, Inbox capture with Cmd-Shift-T, stale-task refusal with multi-page bulk updates, and date controls through `Task::set_date`.
Status: in_progress

### NR19: Build task and project workflows

What to do: Projects shows Active (at least one open action), Inactive, and All with counts and remembers the choice (`957da33d4766`). Add Due/Inbox/Next/Waiting/Someday/Projects/Done views, capture, selection, date controls, missing-next-action indicators, and keyboard task commands.
Constraints: Source edits, card edits, embeds, and AI task writes share NR18 commands. Keep task capture drafts local until submitted.
Write set: task/project views and editor action bindings.
Dependencies: NR16, NR18.
Verify: Native capture/triage/project flows, bulk updates, focus retention, save failures, task shortcuts in Vim and non-Vim views, and 5x result paging.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` 0.9.57 (`5ea5c22310ff`), `src/ui/TasksPage.tsx`, `src/core/projects.ts`, the task methods of `src/core/store.ts` (`updateTask`, `updateTasks`, `setTaskDate`, `captureTask`), `src/core/workspace.ts` (`taskView`, `taskProject`, `projectStatus`), and `tests/tasks-page.test.tsx`.

Index. `quire_core::tasks::Page::undated_tasks` returns each task with its `DateSources` (created and due stamp targets, its own dated links other than the created stamp, and its ancestors' links); `Page::tasks` resolves them, so the frozen vectors still cover the split. The query index stores task rows (format 4, analyzer version 5) with those sources as JSON plus derived `created` and `due` columns that `apply_layout` recomputes when the layout changes, the same pattern as page and link dates; no page is reparsed. A daily page's date is joined at query time from the live page. `overlay_tasks` and the `live_tasks` view give open pages' unsaved tasks. `QueryIndex::task_board` returns every list's count, project summaries (`projectPages` with open, done, and next counts and the first open state), and one filtered list ordered as the reference orders it (path by ICU collation, then position, then the reference `datedFirst`), in one worker step. Bug found and fixed: releasing an overlay left its `overlay_front_tags` rows, so the second overlay of an open page with front matter tags failed on a unique constraint and that page's live analysis stopped; `task_rows_keep_date_sources_through_layout_changes_and_overlays` fails without the fix.

Writes. `crate::page_writes` is the multi-page path bulk tag edits used, extracted so tag edits and task changes share it: read each page from its open buffer or stored head, refuse if an open page changed, save open pages first and hold, `save_many_if_heads`, then `absorb`. `crate::task_edit::update` re-reads every touched page, finds each listed task with `find_current` (block ID or key plus unchanged raw text), plans with `Task::changes` or `Task::set_date`, and writes all pages or none; `task_edit::capture` is the reference `captureTask` into the Inbox page, created when missing, with the project link chosen as the reference chooses it. `TaskContext` is now installed by the stored window with the notebook's clock, time zone, daily layout, and a title lookup over open pages, so task commands in editors stamp by the configured layout and skip the created stamp on today's daily page.

View. `navigation::tasks::TasksView` (Zed `ui` buttons, `DropdownMenu`/`ContextMenu` for project choice, `uniform_list` rows, `Live` for stale-reply rejection) is the Tasks browser destination (`BrowserView::Tasks`), wired in `editor_host/stored/task_views.rs` and refreshed on `IndexChanged`. Keys: Command/Ctrl-Shift-T opens Tasks with capture focused (Enter captures, Escape returns to the list); in the list arrows, Home, End, Enter, Shift-Enter; Command/Ctrl-Enter ticks and Command/Ctrl-Shift-Enter cycles the highlighted task (also while the filter has focus); Space and Shift-Space select, Escape clears; 1 to 5 move to Inbox, Next, Waiting, Someday, Done; D, C, and Shift-D edit the due, created, and completed date; `[` and `]` switch lists; `/` focuses the filter; R reviews the highlighted project. `quire_core::workspace::TaskChoices` remembers the list, project filter, and project status per notebook.

Deviations from `rt`, chosen deliberately. Bulk moves are all-or-nothing: rt applies unchanged pages and names changed ones, Quire refuses the whole move and names the pages, matching the multi-page write contract. Changes written to an open page from the list merge into its buffer outside undo history (`absorb`), where rt adds one undo step per page. Dates are typed (YYYY-MM-DD, `today`, `tomorrow`, `yesterday`, `+N`, `-N`, a weekday; `quire_core::tasks::date_phrase`) instead of a native date picker, which GPUI lacks. State changes use number keys and a bulk toolbar, and since batch M also a per-row state menu (below). Project status uses three toggle buttons. Rows show plain text, page title, state, and date chips, not rendered rich text. Selection covers loaded rows. The capture draft lives in the view for the session and is not written to the workspace file; the filter text and page size are not remembered either. Upcoming is kept, as rt has it.

Tests: `quire_core/tests/tasks.rs` (`undated_tasks_keep_date_sources_that_any_layout_resolves`, `typed_dates_read_relative_phrases_weekdays_and_iso_dates`), `quire_core/tests/workspace.rs` (`task_choices_survive_save_and_load_and_forget_deleted_projects`), `quire_store/tests/index.rs` (`task_rows_keep_date_sources_through_layout_changes_and_overlays`: layout change without reparsing, daily page dates, overlay replacement), `query_index::task_tests` through the production analyzer (Due, Next, and Done counts and order, Upcoming with `-#tag`, projects from front matter, links, and `#project` blocks with the project filter), and `editor_host/stored/tasks_tests.rs` driving keys: `task_lists_count_order_and_change_tasks_in_place`, `selected_tasks_move_together_and_stale_selections_are_refused` (an open page with unsaved typing and a closed page in one move; a closed page saved by another writer refuses the next move and writes nothing), `date_controls_set_change_and_clear_task_dates` (including an unreadable date and a completion date on an open task), `capture_creates_the_inbox_page_then_appends_and_links_projects`, `projects_filter_by_status_flag_missing_next_actions_and_remember_the_choice` (Active 1, Inactive 2, All 3, remembered across reopening, Review), `new_projects_start_with_a_next_action_and_open`, and `task_commands_use_the_notebook_daily_layout_and_page_titles`. Stale replies are rejected by `navigation::live::Live`, covered by its unit test. Gates passing on 2026-10-31: both scoped Clippy gates with `-D warnings` (the app with `--features test-support,gpui_platform/runtime_shaders`), `cargo fmt --check`, the full `cargo test -p quire -p quire_core -p quire_store` gate run twice (437 tests, 238 in the app, both runs green), and the reference Vitest run (90 tests in 4 files).

Batch C. `LinkedTasks` (`navigation/linked_tasks.rs`) lists tasks on other pages that link to the shown page, directly or through a parent block (`QueryIndex::linked_tasks`), above its linked references, with All, Open, Due, and Done; any status but All replaces the references with the matching tasks, which tick with the checkbox or Cmd/Ctrl-Enter and open at their block; the choice lasts for the session, as rt keeps it in memory. Tasks now remembers its filter text, rows shown, and capture draft with the list and project, offers How tasks work (opens Help), and ends with the reference status line (Saving locally… while a task change is written, then Saved locally). Opening a task reveals its block, and capture into today's daily page follows the Settings choice (`capture_follows_the_settings_choice_to_todays_page_without_a_date`). Tests: `references_tests::linked_tasks_filter_above_references_tick_and_open`, `quire_store/tests/index.rs::linked_tasks_come_from_other_pages_linking_by_any_name`, `tasks_tests::the_status_line_says_saving_until_a_task_change_is_written`, and the reopen test in NR16.

Batch F. Task rows in Tasks and linked tasks are rich text with tag tones (NR17 batch F notes), and their links open. `tasks_tests::a_failed_task_save_writes_nothing_says_why_and_keeps_focus_for_a_retry` holds the notebook database with another connection's `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`: Cmd/Ctrl-Enter then shows "Cannot begin a notebook transaction. Keep the editor contents, check available space and write permissions, and retry after other writers finish.", writes nothing, keeps the task listed and the list focused, and the retry after the lock clears ticks it with focus still on the list.
Batch M (2026-10-31, `f0353af7b0a9`, rt `TasksPage.tsx` 805 per-card `select`). Each task row's state label is a Zed `PopoverMenu` whose `ContextMenu` is built only when opened, lists the five states with the current one ticked and selected, and moves that task alone through `TasksView::set_state` (the shared `run`, so all-or-nothing page writes and the "Moved to Waiting." notice), whatever else is selected. As after every change, the selection then clears. Test: `tasks_tests::a_rows_state_menu_moves_that_task_alone` (real clicks on the row's menu and its Waiting entry, with another task selected and left in place).
Remaining: work counts at 5x notebook size (rows page by 40, but counts read every live task row per refresh). NR19.1 live task lists are done (see its proof notes).
Status: in_progress

#### NR19.1: Render live task lists

What to do: Render a block holding only `{{tasks: QUERY}}` or `{{[[tasks]]: QUERY}}` as a live list of matching tasks that can be ticked in place; `{{tasks}}` means `here`. Support states (`inbox`, `next`, `waiting`, `someday`, `done`, `open`, `due`, `upcoming`, `all`, default `open`), `#tag`, `[[Page]]`, `here`, `project:Name`, words and quoted words with `-` negation, `by:page`, `by:project`, `by:state`, `sort:due`, and `limit:N` (default 50). Show a malformed query's error in place. On the Templates page, show the line as written.
Constraints: Results come from indexed task queries with the live overlay. Ticking uses NR18 commands through the source page's shared buffer, and the query text stays unchanged.
Dependencies: NR12, NR15, NR18.
Verify: Every case in `rt/tests/fixtures/task-queries.json`, which the Go terminal app also runs, plus ports of `task-query` and the editor test that ticks a task through the list.
Proof notes: Source: `rt` 0.9.57 (`5ea5c22310ff`; `src/core/task-query.ts` and `src/core/projects.ts` are unchanged since `42de3c8949e1`), `src/ui/TaskQueryEmbed.tsx`, the `TaskQueryWidget` placement in `src/editor/Editor.tsx`, `tests/task-query.test.ts`, `tests/fixtures/task-queries.json`, and the two `{{tasks: …}}` cases of `tests/editor.test.tsx`.

Core. `quire_core::task_query` ports `taskQueryLine` (`query_line`, on a block's first line with its `^id` stripped, also returning where `{{...}}` sits), `parseTaskQuery` (`TaskQuery::parse`, sharing `query_tokens` with the reference filter, with UTF-16 length rules and JavaScript `Number` semantics for `limit:`, so `limit:0x3`, `limit:1e1`, and `limit:2.0` are accepted and `limit:1.5` or `limit:1_0` refused with the reference message), and `runTaskQuery` (`run`). `run` takes tasks, project pages and each task's membership, a page resolver, and a title collator as inputs instead of a Notebook, so the store and tests supply them. `tests/reference/core/task-query.ts` and `core/projects.ts` are frozen rt copies whose only change is the Notebook type import, pointing at `core/query-notebook.ts`, which names the six members they read; hashes are in `tests/reference/UPSTREAM.json` (`task_query_extractions`). `tools/task-query-vectors.ts` runs them over 11 synthetic pages and 90 seeded tasks: 338 queries (an edge for every term, JavaScript whitespace, Unicode case, the `Number` forms, and 300 seeded combinations) and 22 query lines, byte-identical in UTC and America/New_York. `crates/quire_core/tests/task_query.rs` checks every parsed field, every group's label, page, and task order, and every total against them, computing membership with the already ported `memberships`.

Index. `QueryIndex::task_list` runs a query over the live task rows (unsaved edits included) in one worker step, resolving `[[Page]]` terms and task links through `resolve_page` with a per-query cache and reading project membership only when a term needs it; `task_board` and `task_list` now share `page_finder` and `project_members`. `query_index::task_tests::task_lists_match_the_shared_fixture` runs all 30 cases of rt `tests/fixtures/task-queries.json` (copied unchanged, hash recorded) through the production analyzer and index.

Write. `crates/quire/src/task_lists.rs` follows the NR15 embed mechanism. `editor_projection::apply_blocks` finds blocks whose first line is one query (not on `Templates.md`, matching rt `isTemplatesPage`), drops link and preview decorations inside the braces as rt does, and passes the spots to `task_lists::sync`, which keeps a list whose query and place are unchanged and otherwise inserts a Zed custom block placed Below the line holding a `TaskListView` GPUI entity, one line per row. The `{{...}}` text folds into a header (`☰ Tasks · next by:page · 2`, `· 1 of 2` past the limit, `· query error`) while the caret is outside the block and shows as written while the caret is in it, through the same `shown` rule as embed headers; clicking the header puts the caret there. Rows: a checkbox (toggle), the task text (opens its page, beside with Shift), a state button (moves Inbox, Next, Waiting, Someday, and reopens Done into Inbox, as Cmd-Shift-Enter does), the page title, and a due chip; group labels for page and project groups open their page; Show more tasks adds 50. Each view queries through `Live`, so superseded requests and older index generations never apply; `references_changed` (driven by `IndexChanged` through the reference host) requeries every list with the editor's page as `here` and today's date from `TaskContext`. A task on the editor's own page changes through `tasks::change_listed`: a fresh analysis of the buffer, `find_current` refusing a changed task, and one `Editor::transact`, so it is one undo step in that editor; a task elsewhere, or outside an embedded excerpt, goes through `task_edit::update` and `page_writes` (all pages or none). The reference `Notebook` trait gains `page_path`, `show_page`, and `writers`.

Deviations from `rt`, chosen deliberately. Only the `{{...}}` text folds; the bullet and line stay, where rt replaces the whole line with its widget. The list stays visible while the caret reveals the query, so editing the query shows its effect live; rt removes the widget while the caret is on the line. Rows carry a state button where rt shows date inputs; date controls are not in the rows. Ticking another page's task merges into that page's open buffer outside undo (`absorb`), as Tasks browser changes do, where rt adds an undo step to that page. Rows show plain text, not rendered rich text with tag tones. A query naming `here` in an editor whose page is not loaded yet matches nothing until the next refresh.

Bug found and fixed. Asking which page an editor shows while Write binds can happen inside a `StoredWindow` update, and `WindowNotebook` read the window entity there and panicked; it now reads open pages through the session (`Rc<RefCell<NotebookSession>>`), as task titles already did. The existing `stored_recovery_draws_in_two_read_only_views_with_conflicts_and_tombstones` failed without the fix and is its regression test. A test-side lesson: a GPUI click is lost when a reply redraws the list between press and release, so the tests click only after the index has analyzed every save and every list has settled (`task_lists::settled`).

Tests: `quire_core/tests/task_query.rs` (vectors, query lines), `query_index::task_tests::task_lists_match_the_shared_fixture`, and `editor_host/stored/task_lists_tests.rs` with real clicks and keys: the port of rt's editor case (grouped `next by:page` list with Garden and Launch, the `colour:red` error in place, exact folded display, ticking a task on a closed page through page writes with the stamped text in the store and the query text unchanged); ticking and cycling on the same page with exact buffer text, one undo per change, and the list following, Vim on (`escape u`) and off (Cmd/Ctrl-Z); refreshing after another writer saves, `1 of 2`, and Show more; the caret revealing and refolding the query with `j`/`k` and with arrows; and `Templates.md` keeping the query as written. Commands: the full `cargo test -p quire -p quire_core -p quire_store --locked --features quire/test-support,gpui_platform/runtime_shaders` passed twice in a row (463 tests, 261 in the app), the task list tests passed five consecutive runs, both Clippy gates with `-D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`, and the `tests/reference` Vitest run (90 tests) pass. No vendored Zed file changed.

Batch F. Keyboard access: `task_lists::init` registers a `ToggleTask` handler on every editor ahead of `tasks::init`'s, so Cmd/Ctrl-Enter with the caret in a query block moves focus into its list instead of making the query line a task. The focused list carries the `QuireControl QuireList QuireTaskQueryList` contexts, so page editor and Vim bindings (all constrained `!QuireControl`) never act on the page behind it; it answers the list keys (rows with a task, a page group, or Show more are stops), Cmd/Ctrl-Enter ticks, Cmd/Ctrl-Shift-Enter moves to the next state, D opens the due date, and Escape returns to the page. Rows carry a due date button in place of the plain chip: it opens a single-line date field (the Tasks browser's `field_editor` and `date_phrase`) whose `QuireTaskQueryDate > Editor` context binds Enter, Escape, and the few editing keys a date needs; a set or cleared date goes through `task_edit::update` and page writes, so on the list's own page it merges outside undo, as Tasks browser dates do. Task text is rich text whose links open through the editor's notebook. Tests in `task_lists_tests.rs`: `the_keyboard_reaches_list_rows_ticks_moves_and_dates_them_with_vim` and `_with_vim_off` (Cmd/Ctrl-Enter on the query line leaving it as written, Down and tick, Up and cycle, D with an unreadable date refused in place then `tomorrow` stamped, Escape back to the page), `a_list_inside_an_embed_lists_the_embedded_page_as_here`, and `ticking_a_task_whose_page_is_open_in_another_tab_updates_that_tab` (the other tab's open buffer and the store both ticked, the query unchanged).
Remaining: none. Rows still carry a state button rather than rt's state select, and a due date set on the list's own page is not an undo step in that editor.
Status: done


### NR20: Port the Templates page and placeholders

What to do: Read templates only from `Templates.md` at the notebook root, opened from a pinned sidebar entry that creates it with the Meeting, Project, Person, and My Day starters. Each top-level block with indented children is a template named by its text. Add `jj` completion with narrowing, and expand `{cursor}`, `{today}`, `{date: phrase}` (today, tomorrow, yesterday, `+N`, `-N`, a weekday, or YYYY-MM-DD), `{time}`, `{weekday}`, `{page}`, `{ask: question}` (asked once per question), and `{pick: #tag}`. The picker lists names used before in blocks with that tag, newest first with use counts, narrows by fuzzy match, and offers a Create row. Show the guide with its fourteen examples and Add buttons outside the page text.
Constraints: Copy every other brace form, including `{{tasks: ...}}` and `{{query: ...}}`, as written. Roam SmartBlock markers and commands are no longer read; imported JavaScript stays inert. Inserted blocks get fresh IDs with internal links following, the trigger block keeps its ID, and insertion is one undo step. An unknown placeholder, a dismissed question, or a changed template stops before editing. Inject the clock and picker input for deterministic tests.
Write set: template core, Templates page and guide, `jj` and picker UI, expansion fixtures.
Dependencies: NR15, NR18.
Verify: Port the `templates`, `templates-page`, `template-pick`, `fuzzy`, and editor `jj` tests: discovery only on the Templates page, every placeholder and date phrase, `pastNames` ranking, live lists copied, cancellation, fresh IDs, refusal of a changed template, starter and example expansion with no placeholder left, and `{pick}` working when the tagged pages are not loaded.
Proof notes: The source contract changed in `rt` `fc05bce17c2f` and `a153d8aa7ffb`; `plans/templates-page.md` supersedes `plans/roam-template-commands.md`. Template discovery needs only `Templates.md` and the tag-scoped `pastNames` query. Source: `rt` 0.9.57 (`5ea5c22310ff`), `src/core/templates.ts`, `src/core/template-guide.ts`, `src/core/fuzzy.ts`, `src/editor/template-pick.ts`, `src/editor/template-input.ts`, `src/ui/TemplatesGuide.tsx`, `Notebook.openTemplates` in `src/core/store.ts`, the `jj` branch of `complete` in `src/editor/Editor.tsx`, and `tests/templates.test.ts`, `tests/fuzzy.test.ts`, `tests/template-pick.test.ts`, `tests/template-input.test.ts`, `tests/templates-page.test.tsx`.

Core. `quire_core::templates` ports `isTemplatesPage`, `templatesInPage`, `templateDate`, `copyWithNewIds`, the placeholder fill and block render of `expandTemplate`, `preserveTemplateRoot`, `pastNames`, and the editor's caret mapping and indentation (`placement`); `quire_core::templates::guide` holds the eight placeholders, four rules, fourteen examples, and starters; `quire_core::fuzzy` ports `fuzzyMatch` and `fuzzyFilter`. Every function takes the blocks and references of the text it reads, so the native analyzer feeds the app and rt's parser feeds the vectors. Expansion is resumable: `expand` returns the first question without an answer, the caller asks it and calls again, so questions come in rt's order, each once, and a bad date after a question fails after asking, as in rt. The placeholder pattern uses ASCII-only case folding (`(?i-u:…)`) to match JavaScript's non-Unicode `i` flag. Names sort with the ICU root collator (moved from a dev-dependency to a dependency of `quire_core`; `Cargo.lock` is unchanged). `DailyCalendar::clock_at` gives the local date, hour, and minute for `{time}`. Frozen copies: `tests/reference/core/templates.ts` (imports repointed to the frozen markdown and daily copies and to `core/template-notebook.ts`), `template-guide.ts` and `fuzzy.ts` unchanged; hashes in `tests/reference/UPSTREAM.json` (`template_extractions`). `tests/reference/templates.test.ts` reruns rt's expectations over the frozen modules. `tools/template-vectors.ts` (`bun tools/template-vectors.ts`; output byte-identical with `TZ=America/New_York`) writes 103 Templates pages (42 handwritten edges, the starters, all fourteen examples, 60 seeded) with 821 expansions over three clocks, two daily layouts, two destinations, and dismissed questions, block IDs coming from a counter in place of `crypto.randomUUID`, plus 114 date phrases, 10 past-name cases, 512 fuzzy matches with exact scores, and the guide. `crates/quire_core/tests/templates.rs` checks definitions, copies, every question asked, text and UTF-16 caret, preserved roots, dates, past names, fuzzy scores and order, the guide, no placeholder left in any starter or example, refusal of a changed template, the clock in three zones, and placement.

App. `crates/quire/src/templates.rs` feeds core from the editor's block analysis (`definitions`) and from detached snapshots parsed off the main thread (`parse`). The sidebar's pinned Templates entry (`editor_host/stored/template_views.rs`) asks the index for a root page whose path is `templates.md` in any case, opening it, or creates `Templates.md` with the starters through `create_page_with`; it shows as active while the main pane shows that page. `templates/guide.rs` is a GPUI view beside the page, outside its text, listing the rules, placeholders, and examples; Add appends a definition through the page's shared buffer (autosaved like any edit) and examples the page defines show Added. `jj` is a branch of the NR15 `CompletionProvider`: a line typed up to the caret matching rt's trigger (indentation, an optional list marker, then `jj`), outside code blocks, lists the Templates page's current templates (read through its shared buffer, so unsaved edits count) by name; Zed's menu narrows them by the text after `jj`, and Enter or Tab confirms. The completion inserts nothing; its `confirm` callback starts `templates/insert.rs`, which refuses a trigger block with children, re-reads the page and refuses a changed template, copies the body with fresh `b-<uuid>` IDs (links to `Templates#^id` follow to the destination), asks questions through `Notebook::prompt`, keeps the trigger block's ID on the first inserted block, and replaces the trigger line with one `Editor::transact` bracketed by `finalize_last_transaction`, under `ViewEditIntent::Structural`, through anchors so excerpt editors map too. It refuses if the page changed since the choice. Questions are a Zed picker over the window (`templates/prompt.rs`): `{ask}` offers the typed text, `{pick: #tag}` lists `past_names` from `IndexClient::tagged_blocks` (page links read by parsing each snippet with the native analyzer), up to 50, narrowed with `quire_core::fuzzy` and highlighted, with a Create row unless the name exists. Escape cancels; every refusal shows its message through the outline notice (`outline::show_notice` takes composed messages). `{today}`, `{date}`, `{time}`, and `{weekday}` read `TaskContext`'s clock and calendar, and `{page}` its page title, so tests inject both.

Deviations from `rt`, chosen deliberately. Questions are Zed pickers rather than HTML dialogs; an `{ask}` row reads `Insert “…”`. The guide is a column beside the page instead of a section below it, so its Add buttons stay reachable without scrolling the page. Bug fixed: rt trims the space after a final `{cursor}` bullet (`- Standup`'s last line became `  -` with the caret after the dash, so typed text left the list item); `placement` keeps whitespace up to the caret (regression test `a_cursor_on_the_last_bullet_keeps_its_space`, and the pick GPUI test expects `  - `). With Vim, the template is its own undo step inside the Insert session, as in rt: `u` after `escape` first undoes text typed after insertion, then the template, then the trigger.

Tests: `quire_core/tests/templates.rs` (10), `templates::prompt` and `templates::insert` unit tests (rows, Create, trigger pattern), and GPUI tests with real keys and clicks: `template_tests.rs` (the sidebar entry creating `Templates.md` with the starters in the buffer and the store, the entry marked active, the guide's Added marks, Add on a defined example doing nothing, Add appending One-on-one and autosaving it, reopening the same page; a lowercase `templates.md` reused while `Notes/Templates.md` is ignored) and `template_insert_tests.rs` (Vim on in Source: `A j j` listing four templates and not `Other.md`'s, `m e` narrowing to Meeting, one question for two `{ask: With whom?}`, exact text with time, weekday, and page title, the caret at `{cursor}`, and `u` undoing the template as one step; Vim off in Write: `jjmy` inserting My Day with daily links and the live list line, Cmd/Ctrl-Z restoring `- jjmy` and redo; fresh IDs with links following and an unreferenced ID dropped; the trigger block's `^keep` moving to the first inserted block with links to it intact; `{pick: #standup}` listing names from two unopened daily pages, newest first with counts, narrowing to `ops` and creating it; Escape on a question and a template changed while the menu was open both leaving `- jjmee`; no menu inside a fenced block; and the children refusal). The NR19.1 test keeping `{{tasks}}` as written on `Templates.md` still passes. Commands: the full `cargo test -p quire -p quire_core -p quire_store --locked --features quire/test-support,gpui_platform/runtime_shaders` passed twice in a row on the branch (498 tests, 280 in the app) and twice again after merging NR13 and NR06 from `main` (523 tests, 288 in the app); both Clippy gates with `-D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`, and the `tests/reference` Vitest run (100 tests) pass. No vendored Zed file changed. A test-side lesson: the Templates entry test lost its click twice under parallel load, because a GPUI test click runs the executor between press and release and an index status line or a save reply appearing then moves the sidebar under the pointer; `template_tests::click` now waits for a quiet window (index caught up, no status line, every open page saved and idle), after which six consecutive full app runs passed. Two unrelated flakes were seen under the same load and pass alone: `editor_projection_tests::focused_link_editing_and_copying_use_the_original_source` (asserts right after keystrokes without waiting for the parse; no completion provider there) and `embeds_tests::the_caret_reveals_an_embed_reference_its_header_opens_the_source_and_source_view_drops_embeds` (a header click, the same lost-click pattern); both predate NR20.

Batch B follow-up.
